# Makeborne launch production plan

Written 2026-10-26 by the head of creative production, for the owner and whoever edits. **Status: plan only. Nothing was bought, generated, signed up for or posted.** The machine-readable version is `data/production.json` (same content, plus every variable). Template scaffolds are in `production/`. Evidence labels: [F] fact from a cited source, [V] vendor or agency data, [U] unverified, [O] owner decision.

## 0. The short version (read this first)

### How we make videos (one screen)

Every video shows the real product. Higgsfield adds short cinematic scenes around it: an opening shot, a moment in the middle, the scene behind the end card, or a slow camera move over a real result. HyperFrames puts every word, timer and the brand on top. Nothing generated is ever shown as Makeborne itself.

**1. Record the screen, add the words** (42% of what we make). Speedruns, demos, tutorials, before and after, 'made from one brief'. About 2 hours the first time, 1.5 hours after that.

1. Type a brief into Makeborne and record the screen while it builds. Never stop the recording.
2. Record one change in chat, then publish or export.
3. Open the matching template, drop the recording in, type the words that appear on screen.
4. If you sped the build up, the little clock says so ('Sped up ×8').
5. Record your voice over it, add captions, check it on your phone, post.

**2. Talk to the phone** (15% of what we make). Day 1, Day 7, Day 30, 'why paid plans only', 'who's behind this'. About 1.5 hours.

1. Phone at eye level, window light, say the five lines. Three takes.
2. Keep the best take, cut the pauses.
3. Add the 'Day N' tag, the real numbers (typed from Whop, never rounded up) and captions.
4. Show screen clips in the bottom half only, never over your face.
5. LinkedIn gets the captioned version without music.

**3. Picture posts** (40% of what we make). Statics, carousels, stories, X cards, link previews. About 25-45 minutes, 1.5-2 hours for a carousel.

1. Take a screenshot of a real Makeborne result.
2. Open the picture template, paste the exact headline from the plan.
3. One coloured italic word, the price and the refund line at the bottom.
4. Save one image per page, check it at phone size.
5. Post, or join the pages into a PDF for LinkedIn.

**4. Add a Higgsfield scene** (15 videos now, 5 more when their feature is live; 1-4 scenes each). Opening shots (the café at dawn, the pitch room, the bookshelf), a moment in the middle, the end card over a scene, a slow camera move over a real result, Black Friday and the holidays. About 10 minutes per shot in Higgsfield, plus 20-30 minutes per video in HyperFrames.

1. Open the shot card in Library › Production and copy the prompt. Upload only what the card says: never unfinished features, client work or customer data.
2. In Higgsfield pick the model and camera move on the card: 9:16, 1080p. Make the takes it says (usually 3).
3. Keep the first good take: no people, no letters, nothing that looks like our app. Save it with its prompt and seed.
4. Drop it into the HyperFrames slot the sheet names (opening, middle, end) and type the words in HyperFrames.
5. The 'AI-generated scene' label stays on for every second of the scene. Switch on the platform's AI label when you post.

The rest is creators (they film themselves, we check the draft) and the two brand films. Founder, creator, tutorial and proof videos stay real-only on purpose.

**The rules:** Only show what is live today. Never say free or trial. No fake customers, ever: no AI people at all. Every sped-up build says so on screen. Every Higgsfield second carries the 'AI-generated scene' label, and nothing generated looks like our app.

**Money:** Higgsfield Plus, $49 for the first month, bought by the owner himself. It is separate from the $1,000 ads budget and never mixed with it. Details: section 12.

**The mix:** In a video with Higgsfield: about two thirds real product, a quarter Higgsfield scenes, the rest HyperFrames cards. Every word is HyperFrames. Every shot and its prompt: section 3b.

## 1. The stack and why

**Recommendation: HyperFrames + real captures as the base ($0/month) plus Higgsfield Plus monthly ($49, production budget, owner signs up Tue 27 Oct). Keep, downgrade to Starter or cancel at the Sun 22 Nov review by the measured lift.** The product is the proof, so real captures carry every video. Higgsfield adds the cinematic seconds a screen recording cannot (the shop at dawn, the pitch room, the bookshelf, the seasonal beats), labelled and never showing product UI. HyperFrames sets every word, timer and the brand exactly and assembles the clips with the real captures. The founder's face carries trust.

- **Real screen captures are the backbone.** Conversion content for AI tools shows the visible UI, the finished artifact and a next step [V/U] (https://www.lightreel.ai/blogs/ai-prosumer-tools-on-tiktok, 2026-10-24); text-plus-product statics lead Meta hit rates [V] (https://motionapp.com/library/research/creative-benchmarks-2026/top-asset-types, updated 2026-04-17); fully AI-made versions of the same ads did worse on sales impact than human-made ones [F] (https://news.syr.edu/2026/05/18/newhouse-research-finds-ai-ads-fall-short-on-sales-impact/, 2026-05-18); and the AI tells (uncanny visuals, fast montages that hide flaws) are what get ads pulled [F] (https://www.nngroup.com/articles/ai-ad/, 2025-12-19). No controlled public study of screen recording versus AI b-roll for SaaS exists (searched 26 Oct 2026; vendor opinions only [V] (https://www.arcade.software/post/ai-video-for-marketing-demos, 2026 (read 2026-10-26))).
- **HyperFrames does the whole edit.** Every overlay letter is HTML, so type is exact; speed chips, captions, format variants and stills come from the same projects; it renders locally for $0 and the v9/v10/v12-v14 films prove the pipeline.
- **The founder is the trust engine.** Day-N videos, the voice on every speedrun, no synthetic voice.
- **Higgsfield is a planned part of production** (its own budget, not the $1,000): the cinematic seconds around the real product (scroll-stopping hooks, mood scenes, transitions, seasonal beats and camera moves over real outputs of live features), always labelled. Not for product shots, fake outputs, people, avatars, UGC ads or testimonials.
- **The mix:** In a video with Higgsfield: about 60-70% real product footage, 20-30% Higgsfield scenes, the rest HyperFrames-only cards; every letter, timer and the brand is HyperFrames, over everything. Measured on the plan: 70% real, 28% Higgsfield, 2% HyperFrames-only cards across the 15 Higgsfield videos. Across all 60 videos (3023 s, including the founder, creator, tutorial and proof videos that stay real-only on purpose) Higgsfield is about 3.6% of posted seconds. 0 generated product images. Of the 48 creatives, 36 are real screenshots composed in HyperFrames, 8 are typeset HyperFrames stills, 4 are built inside Makeborne itself; C28 and C30 add a Higgsfield frame grab behind the type in their Higgsfield version.

### Tools

| Tool | Role | Use for | Plan and cost | Rights notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| HyperFrames CLI 0.8.205 (npx hyperframes) | core | every composition, overlay, caption track, end card and still; format variants (9:16, 4:5, 1:1, 16:9) via production/tools/make-formats.mjs; word-timed captions (`transcribe`), loudness (`normalize-audio`), beat grid (`beats`); stills via `snapshot --at` (statics, carousel pages, covers, OG, thumbnails); batch episodes via `render --batch` (V29, V04 covers) | Local render ($0/month) | Our own HTML compositions. Registry blocks install as editable source in the project. Render locally on an SSD with at least 20 GB free, never on the network drive (v14 draft 2 failed on a network-drive rename). |
| OBS Studio | core | desktop screen capture of the real studio at 2x UI scale, 60 fps, cursor highlight; two capture widths: 1440x900 window (16:9 cuts) and 860x1080 portrait window (vertical-first composer shots) | Free (GPL) ($0/month) | Our own recordings of our own product. |
| Phone camera and native screen recorder | core | founder face-to-camera (eye level, window light, 4K30 or 1080p60); client-side review page on a real phone (V05, V12, V13, V46); real B-roll: coffee, kitchen hands, Sarajevo street, bedroom light (never a child's face) | Existing ($0/month) | Consent from anyone on camera; no customer data on screen. |
| Makeborne (clean owner account on production) | core | every product shot and every output shown; decks and documents that are themselves the creative (C07, C20, C38, C45, V39, V43) | Owner account ($0/month) | Our product and outputs. Fictional, trademark-checked business names only. AI provider cost of brand builds sits outside the $1,000 (plan.json outsideBudget: about $180; decision D14). |
| PowerPoint (desktop, light theme) | core | V03, V06, V23, V35, V41 and C17/C18: open the exported .pptx, select the chart, Edit Data | Existing licence assumed [U] ($0/month) | Capture the window only: no file path, user name or other apps. |
| Platform music libraries: TikTok Commercial Music Library, Meta Sound Collection, YouTube Audio Library | core | brand TikTok: CML only (business account rule); Instagram/Facebook and YouTube: their own cleared libraries; LinkedIn and X: no music; captions carry the video | Free ($0/month) | Use each library only on its own platform unless its licence says otherwise; log the track ID per post in the rights log. |
| Mixkit 'Opalescent' (already in v9 and v14) | existing | V01 and V50 on Instagram, LinkedIn, X, YouTube organic | Free licence (as recorded in the film notes) ($0/month) | Licence text could not be read on 26 Oct [U]. Re-read before any paid use; on TikTok use a CML track instead. |
| HyperFrames transcribe (local) and Kokoro TTS (`npx hyperframes tts`, local) | core | word-timed captions from the founder's VO; scratch VO for timing only; posted VO is the founder's own voice | Free, on device ($0/month) | A synthetic voice would need YouTube's description label (non-music AI audio) and an on-screen note; the plan avoids it by using the founder's voice. |
| ffmpeg | core | re-encode captures with a keyframe every second (-g 60 at 60 fps) so seeking never freezes; loudness check (-14 LUFS integrated, -1 dBTP true peak); contact sheets | Free ($0/month) | - |
| Native editors and schedulers: TikTok Studio / web scheduler, Meta Business Suite, YouTube Studio, LinkedIn and X schedulers | core | posting, covers, the TikTok reply-to-comment bubble and Stitch (in-app only); platform AI-label and paid-partnership toggles | Free ($0/month) | Platform terms. |
| Higgsfield (web app: Kling 3.0, Cinema Studio camera presets, image-to-video; Seedance 2.0 as fallback) | core (production budget) | scroll-stopping hooks (first 1-3 s), mood scenes, transitions and seasonal beats: text-free, no people, no product UI; image-to-video camera moves over REAL Makeborne outputs of live features (published sites, slides, book pages from the marketing account), kept only if every letter matches; 25 planned shots (4 only once their feature is live) for 15 videos now and 5 beat videos, plus frame grabs for C28 and C30; nothing that shows a person, a face, a logo, readable text or a real business | Plus, monthly: $49 for 1,000 credits (Higgsfield's blog, 24 Oct 2026; Krea lists $59 / 1,200: checkout is final, cap $59). Owner signs up himself. Keep / Starter / cancel at Sun 22 Nov. ($49/month) | Paid plans: commercial use, no watermark. Free plan: watermark, not for posting. Standard terms let Higgsfield train on inputs and outputs: never upload unreleased product UI, client material, customer data or creator footage; image-to-video start frames are only outputs of live features from the clean marketing account, made for the post itself. No IP indemnity below Enterprise. Label every generated second (production/shared/components/ai-label.html). |
| Existing film-library image script (the one that made the v13/v14 stills) | optional | only if a still plate is needed for a cinematic hook (cheaper than video: then animate it with a HyperFrames camera move); the quality bar for the images beat (never posted as product output) | Pay per image, about $0.06 per 2560x1440 high-quality still (v14 run log) ($0/month) | Concept art: label it; never show it as Makeborne output. |
| ElevenLabs | not-recommended-now | only if a faceless VO series is ever approved (none in this plan) | Starter about $6/month (commercial licence); free tier is non-commercial ($0/month) | Cloned or synthetic voice: disclose in the description on YouTube; never clone anyone but the founder, and only with the founder's consent. |
| CapCut | not-recommended | nothing in this plan; HyperFrames covers captions, cuts and covers, and TikTok's in-app editor covers reply bubbles | Pro about $19.99/month [U] ($0/month) | Reported terms give a broad licence to uploaded content [U]: do not upload unreleased product footage, client work or creator footage. Pro templates watermark exports. |

### Higgsfield, researched (26 Oct 2026)

- **Products:** Video generation hub with third-party models inside (Kling 3.0, Seedance 2.0/2.5, Veo 3 / 3 Fast, Wan, Minimax and others) plus its own Cinema Studio (camera, lens, focal length and lighting controls; 70+ camera presets reported, e.g. crash zoom, dolly, 360 orbit, bullet time; clips up to 30 s in Cinema Studio 4.0); Soul image models (Soul 2.0; Soul ID character training from 3-5 photos); Speak / Lipsync Studio (talking avatars, lip-sync); Marketing Studio: product-URL-to-ad ('Click to Ad'), UGC avatar ads, 20+ templates, up to 15 s per generation; Upscaling, Canvas, Supercomputer agent, CLI/MCP and a pay-as-you-go API.
- **Pricing:** Higgsfield's blog (published 24 Oct 2026, read 26 Oct 2026): Starter $15/month 200 credits, Plus $49 1,000, Ultra $129 3,000, Team $79/seat, Scale $215/seat; its 19 Oct 2026 comparison post repeats $15 / $49 / $129. Krea's capture (updated 23 Oct 2026): Starter $19 270, Plus $47 annual / $59 monthly 1,200, Ultra $99 annual / $129 monthly 3,000. Creatify lists lower figures. higgsfield.ai/pricing is JavaScript-rendered and could not be read. Treat the checkout page as final.
- **Credits per generation:** Kling 3.0: 10 credits for 5 s 720p, 12.5 for 5 s 1080p, 37.5 for 15 s 1080p (Higgsfield blog, 19 Oct 2026). Cinema Studio: 25 credits 5 s 720p, 50 for 5 s 1080p (Krea, 23 Oct 2026). Seedance 2.0: about 22-23 credits 5 s 720p, 45-60 credits 5 s 1080p. Veo 3 Fast with audio: about 22 credits 8 s 720p; Veo 3 with audio about 58. Nano Banana Pro: about 2 credits an image.
- **Rights and watermarks:** Commercial use on paid plans; free plan watermarked and not for commercial use; no separate licence; Higgsfield may train on content (Enterprise excluded); IP indemnity only on Enterprise.
- **Limits:** Per model: Veo about 8 s, Kling 3.0 about 10 s, Seedance up to about 15 s, Cinema Studio 4.0 up to 30 s, Marketing Studio 15 s; 720p-1080p typical, some 4K [U: varies by model; check at generation].
- **API:** Yes: api.higgsfield.ai, pay-as-you-go balance ($5 minimum), billed per second of video; web 'unlimited' tiers do not apply.
- **Strengths for short-form ads:** Camera language (presets), many models in one place, cheap short clips on Kling, character consistency tools, fast concept exploration.
- **Weaknesses:** Text and UI cannot be trusted (never render words or screens with it); results vary by model and take several attempts; credits burn quickly on premium models; UGC avatars and talking heads are exactly the AI tell this brand avoids, and an avatar 'customer' is illegal as a testimonial; standard terms allow training on uploads.
- **Verdict for Makeborne:** A planned role with hard limits: the cinematic seconds around the real product (hooks, mood scenes, transitions, seasonal beats) and camera moves over real outputs of live features, always labelled, never people, never text, never product UI.

Sources: https://higgsfield.ai/blog/higgsfield-pricing-explained (2026-10-24 (read 2026-10-26), F (vendor's own blog; the live pricing page is JS-rendered and could not be read)); https://www.krea.ai/blog/higgsfield-pricing-explained-2026-unlimited-credits-and-real-monthly-costs (2026-09-05, updated 2026-10-23 (read 2026-10-26), V (competitor; conflicts with the Higgsfield blog on Starter and Plus)); https://higgsfield.ai/creator-hub/help-center/credits/watermark-and-how-to-remove (2026-08-28 (read 2026-10-26), F); https://higgsfield.ai/creator-hub/help-center/account/who-owns-my-generations-and-can-i-use-them-commercially (read 2026-10-26 (via search summary), F/U); https://higgsfield.ai/creator-hub/help-center/integrations/what-is-the-higgsfield-api (read 2026-10-26 (via search summary), F/U); https://diyai.io/ai-tools/video-generation/reviews/higgsfield-review/ (2026-10-25, V); https://higgsfield.ai/blog/how-to-create-instant-ads (read 2026-10-26 (via search summary), V); https://higgsfield.ai/blog/higgsfield-vs-kling-2026 (2026-10-19 (read 2026-10-26), V (vendor's own blog)); https://higgsfield.ai/camera-controls (read 2026-10-26, V (vendor page)); https://creatify.ai/blog/higgsfield-pricing-(2026)-plans-and-what-you-ll-actually-pay (undated (read 2026-10-26), U (competitor's blog)).

### Alternatives, only where clearly better

- **Veo, Kling, Seedance directly:** no. Higgsfield already exposes them; a second subscription adds cost and a second rights regime, and Higgsfield's camera presets and Cinema Studio are the reason to use it.
- **Runway:** no job in this plan needs it.
- **CapCut for the final edit and captions:** no. HyperFrames covers both; CapCut's reported terms grant a broad licence to uploaded content [U] [U] (https://vidpros.com/capcut-pro-vs-free/, 2026 (read 2026-10-26)), which is wrong for unreleased product and creator footage. TikTok's own editor covers reply bubbles and Stitch.
- **ElevenLabs for voice:** not now. The founder's voice is the brand. If a faceless series is ever approved, Starter is about $6/month with a commercial licence; the free tier is non-commercial [V] (https://bigvu.tv/blog/elevenlabs-pricing-2026-plans-credits-commercial-rights-api-costs/, 2026 (read 2026-10-26)).
- **The existing film-library image script** (made the v13/v14 stills, about $0.06 per 2560x1440 still per the v14 run log): the cheapest way to make a still plate if a hook ever needs one; animate it with a HyperFrames camera move. Never for product imagery.

### Real screen recordings vs AI b-roll: the recommended mix

No controlled public study compares screen-recorded demos with AI b-roll for SaaS conversion (searched 26 Oct 2026). The evidence that exists points one way: conversion content in this category shows the UI, the finished artifact and a next step (LightReel, Oct 2026); text-plus-product statics lead Meta hit rates (Motion 2026); fully AI-made ads underperform human-made versions of the same briefs on sales impact (Ipsos/Syracuse, May 2026); and AI tells (uncanny visuals, fast montages that hide flaws) trigger backlash (NN/G, Dec 2025).

**Mix:** In a video with Higgsfield: about 60-70% real product footage, 20-30% labelled Higgsfield scenes (hooks, mood, transitions, seasonal beats, camera moves over real outputs) and all text, timing and brand in HyperFrames. Founder, creator, tutorial and proof videos stay real-only. Never product proof from a generator. Judge on /go/ clicks and sales per 1,000 views and move the mix weekly.

## 2. Pipelines, step by step (SOPs)

### Capture standards (every pipeline)

- **account:** A clean owner account on production makeborne.com used only for marketing: fictional display name, no real e-mail visible, an empty bookmarks bar, a dedicated browser profile, no other tabs. Crop out the account e-mail, avatar, browser tabs and bookmarks. Never show a Whop store page or any unreleased menu item.
- **desktop:** OBS, 60 fps, CQP/CRF high quality. Two window sizes: 1440x900 CSS px at 2x device scale (records 2880x1800) for 16:9 and punch-ins, and 860x1080 CSS px at 2x (records 1720x2160) for vertical-first composer shots. Browser zoom 100%; the 2x comes from the display scale so text is sharp when punched in. Cursor highlight on; system notifications off.
- **phone:** Native screen recording at 60 fps, Do Not Disturb on, battery and time not distracting. For a physical phone in frame: a second phone on a tripod at eye level, window light, no hand blur.
- **builds:** Record every build uncut from Create to finished, and log brief, start and end time (m:ss) and credits in the run log. The uncut file is kept for the 90-150 s full cut, the landing page and the 'uncut build' DM reply. Punch cuts use data-playback-rate and the clock chip states the factor.
- **names:** Every business name a build invents is checked against real trademarks and local businesses before it appears in a public frame (trademark office search plus a web search). Known traps: Tern, Tidal, 'Northface Labs', Galerie Nord, Kazu, Round Nine, Salt & Swell, Petal & Stem.
- **data:** No personal data on screen: blur (12 px Gaussian in HyperFrames, or `npx hyperframes media-treatment`) every real business name, address, phone and e-mail in lead-finder results; crop Whop screens to totals only; handles blurred unless the person agreed.
- **reencode:** Before editing: `ffmpeg -i in.mp4 -c:v libx264 -crf 16 -g 60 -pix_fmt yuv420p -an out.mp4` (keyframe every second at 60 fps; seeking never freezes).
- **storage:** Captures go to the private asset library on a local disk, never into this public repository (production/.gitignore blocks media).

### P1-speedrun: UI speedrun / workflow demo (real screen capture + HyperFrames timer and kinetic overlay)

Used by: Core format: V02, V03, V04, V07, V10, V20, V25, V27, V28, V41, V42, V51, V52, V55, V56, V57, V58, V59. Template: T02-speedrun (plus T04 triptych for V10, T01 end card where the script ends on one). Time: about 240 min the first time, 110 min after.

1. Write the brief on the run sheet (exact text, fictional name), check names, set the capture standard (2x UI, 60 fps).
2. Record the frame-0 asset first: the finished output as a 390-wide phone scroll (published site) or the finished slide/page.
3. Record the composer typed by hand in the 860x1080 portrait window, Plan, then Create.
4. Record the build uncut; log start/end time and credits. Never stop the recording to retry.
5. Record exactly one chat edit, then Publish and the live Makeborne address in a browser (V02/V04/V57) or Export to PPTX/PDF (decks, books).
6. `node production/tools/new-project.mjs T02-speedrun <id> --episode <n> --formats 4x5,16x9`, drop captures into assets/capture/, re-encode with -g 60.
7. Set the variables: hookLine, briefText (verbatim), clockText ('Real time · m:ss' only if data-playback-rate is 1, else 'Sped up ×N' with the same N), three 1-3 word labels, editRequest (verbatim), liveText with the measured total, ctaText per platform.
8. Record the founder VO to picture (phone voice memo is fine, no music while recording), `npx hyperframes transcribe assets/audio/vo.wav --model small.en`, then `node production/tools/captions-to-html.mjs assets/transcript.json compositions/captions.html --max-words 4 --fix makeborn=Makeborne`.
9. Music: TikTok CML track (brand TikTok) at 0.14 under voice; IG/YT library track; none on LinkedIn/X. Two or three SFX only on visible actions (click on Create, pop on chip landings).
10. `npx hyperframes check --json`, `npx hyperframes snapshot --review` (read review-phone.jpg at 360 px), quality bar (section 7), then render 9:16 at 30 fps (`--quality delivery`) and the format variants.
11. Cut the 60-150 s full build from the same capture (same project, longer build window at 1x or 2x) for TikTok long, LinkedIn 4:5 and the landing page.

### P2-before-after: Before / after (HyperFrames split + capture)

Used by: V06, V14, V30, V48, V49, V60 (and statics C16, C39, C48). Template: T03-split (mode stack on 9:16, side-by-side on 16:9/1:1; onion for V30; before-after-wipe registry component for the wipe). Time: about 180 min the first time, 90 min after.

1. Make the BEFORE honestly and keep its provenance note (our own slides, our own dated site, the v12 real deck before the edit, the launch-week typographic run of the same brief).
2. Capture the AFTER from a real run at identical scroll position and zoom as the BEFORE (same window size, same scroll offset).
3. For 'only that thing changes' pieces, check frame-by-frame with `npx hyperframes compare` or the onion mode: if anything else moved, re-record instead of posting.
4. Labels state measured facts only ('Before: 2 hours' is the founder's own honest time; 'After: real time 3:40' from the run log).
5. Check, snapshot, quality bar, render 9:16 + 4:5 + 16:9 as the platforms need.

### P3-cinematic-hook: Higgsfield scenes (Higgsfield clip -> HyperFrames type and label -> real capture)

Used by: V02, V03, V07, V14, V15, V16, V18, V25, V26, V27, V29, V40, V41, V42, V48 now; V51, V55, V56, V58, V59 once their feature is live; frame grabs for C28 and C30. Template: T07-cinematic-hook slots (hook, bridge, match-to-ui, endplate) and T04 i2v, mounted in the video's own template project; T09 lay-plate for stills. Time: about 60 min the first time, 30 min after.

1. Open the shot card (Library > Production > Higgsfield shots, or higgsfield.shots in data/production.json). Copy the prompt and the negative exactly.
2. Higgsfield: pick the model and camera preset on the card, 9:16, 1080p, the card's duration, no audio. Image-to-video: upload only the card's reference image (a real output of a live feature from the marketing account, made for this post).
3. Make the card's number of takes at most; stop at the first take that passes the keep rule. Record prompt, negative, model, preset, seed, take, credits, plan and date in the sidecar .json and the rights log; download the paid-plan file to the private library.
4. In the video's HyperFrames project, mount the slot the sheet names (perVideo[].template.hfSlots): set plateSrc to the kept take and data-media-start to the section's clip start; type the words in the slot's variables (never let the model render words).
5. Check: the 'AI-generated scene' chip is on for every generated frame; the hard cut lands on the beat; the real capture after the cut is unchanged; hyperframes check --json clean; snapshot --review.
6. Post the Higgsfield cut on TikTok, Reels and Shorts with the TikTok AI-generated content toggle on, YouTube altered or synthetic = Yes and the Meta AI label; LinkedIn and X keep the real-only cut. A/B pairs: the real version keeps its own slot and /go/ code.
7. Judge every Sunday on /go/ clicks and sales per 1,000 views (higgsfield.abTests) and move next week's mix by 5 points toward the winner.

### P4-founder: Founder face-to-camera (phone + captions)

Used by: V08, V09, V11, V19, V21, V31, V37, V38, V39, V43, V44, V45, V47, V54. Template: T05-founder (Day-N pill, ledger count-up, b-roll inserts, captions). Time: about 120 min the first time, 60 min after.

1. Write 4-6 sentences from the script; numbers typed in from Whop and the /go/ report on the day, never estimated or rounded up.
2. Film 3 takes at eye level with window light (phone on a tripod, 1080p60 or 4K30, lavalier or the phone 30 cm away). First words are the hook.
3. Pick the best take, cut breaths and dead air; punch-ins (1.00 -> 1.08) on emphasis words; b-roll inserts in the lower half only, never over the face.
4. Captions are mandatory (LinkedIn plays muted): transcribe, fix names, captions-to-html at 3-5 words per group.
5. Day-N pill for 3 s; the ledger card only with the real figures; crop or blur every buyer name and e-mail in screen inserts.
6. LinkedIn 4:5 1080x1350 with captions and no music; TikTok/Reels 9:16; X 16:9 or 1:1 as the post needs.

### P5-stills: Slideshow, carousel and stills (HyperFrames stills, Figma-free HTML)

Used by: All 48 creatives except the four built inside Makeborne; V16 photo-mode stills; video covers. Template: T09-stills (lay-proof, lay-type, lay-step, lay-end), sizes per creative. Time: about 180 min the first time, 25 min after.

1. Copy the creative's COMPOSE text from data/creatives.json verbatim into the page markup (headline, subcopy, strip). Every letter is HTML; no model redraws UI or type.
2. Drop the real capture (PNG at 2x) into the capture card; for type-only creatives there is no capture.
3. One page per 1-second clip; `npx hyperframes snapshot --at 0.5,1.5,... --output stills/<Cid>` writes one PNG per page.
4. Check at 360 px wide (phone legibility), contrast of #5C616D on canvas for small type, alt text written for each image.
5. LinkedIn documents: join the PNGs into a PDF (or export straight from Makeborne for the built-in ones).
6. Export at the creative's size; JPEG quality 90 for Meta/IG, PNG for X and LinkedIn.

### P6-ugc: UGC creator brief (real creators, real use, disclosed)

Used by: V32-V36 (wave 1), V53 (wave 2, after images ship). Template: T10-ugc-pack (only with the creator's approval, for the Spark/partnership version). Time: about 90 min the first time, 45 min after.

1. Send the one-page brief (content/videos.md, UGC section) and the signed agreement with the 60-day Spark/partnership clause, the comped Studio month, the /go/c-<handle> link, the use-my-brief link and the affiliate code.
2. The creator films their own real use during the comped month: their screen recording, their face, natural light, no filters. No scripts read word for word; only what they actually experienced.
3. Disclosure: the creator says 'Ad' (or 'This is an ad for Makeborne') in the first seconds, 'Ad · ...' on screen for 3 s, '#ad' first in the caption, and the platform paid-partnership toggle on.
4. Founder reviews the draft against the quality bar before it posts (only live features, no 'free', no vendor names, no income claims, label any speed-up).
5. Never: an AI avatar, an AI voice, a 'customer' who does not exist, or our own staff posing as customers. A fake testimonial is banned by the FTC rule (16 CFR 465) and breaks ASA rules on testimonials; it is not allowed here in any form.
6. Pay only after the attribution gate; boost only if the post qualifies (b5 rule).

### P7-showcase: Output showcase (real Makeborne outputs, Ken Burns / parallax in HyperFrames)

Used by: V15, V16, V29, V37 (grid), V10 frame 0 (triptych); covers C27, C28. Template: T04-showcase (scroll, slides, triptych). Time: about 150 min the first time, 30 min after.

1. Run the brief once on production and keep the first output (no cherry-picked re-rolls; if one fails, say so or drop it). Log time and credits.
2. Capture: 390-wide full-page PNG at 2x for scroll episodes; 1440x900 first screen for heroes; book pages and slides at their real ratio.
3. Set the sentence chip verbatim, the footnote from the run log ('Built in 2:41 · 38 credits · unedited').
4. One continuous move per shot (scroll or 1.00 -> 1.04 Ken Burns), never parked, never a wall or grid of cards (owner rejected).
5. Daily series: `render --batch rows.json --output renders/V29-{episode}.mp4` from a row per output.

### P8-tutorial: Tutorial (real capture + VO + step chips)

Used by: V13, V17, V22, V23, V24 (9-minute YouTube). Template: T06-tutorial (9:16 for Shorts/TikTok/Reels; 16:9 with chapter cards for V24). Time: about 200 min the first time, 120 min after.

1. One continuous capture of the whole task at real speed, narrated live or with VO recorded afterwards.
2. Trim each step window from the same recording (data-media-start), the build step sped with the clock chip.
3. Step chips 'Step N: ...' at each step; punch-ins on the control in use.
4. YouTube: chapters in the description, SRT captions uploaded, three 1280x720 thumbnails (C14/C21 pattern) for Test & Compare.

### P9-pov-props: POV, meme and prop skits (honest props + real capture)

Used by: V05, V12, V18, V26, V40, V46. Template: T08-props + T02/T03 for the real product part. Time: about 150 min the first time, 75 min after.

1. Build the prop in HTML with invented names (inbox stack, e-mail thread, notification over a clock, a fake 'MEGA SALE' banner that we cross out). Never a copy of a real inbox or app.
2. Capture the real product part (review link on a real phone, chat fix, Approve, pricing page).
3. Film any hand/over-shoulder texture for real (second phone on a tripod).
4. Comedic timing on the beat; 10-28 s; captions only if there is speech.

### P10-film-recut: Film recut (v9 Fly-over now, v14 'Anything' at the images beat)

Used by: V01, V50. Template: T11-film-recut (+ T01 end card as a sub-composition). Time: about 120 min the first time, 60 min after.

1. Read only from the film library; copy the finished render into the project's assets/capture/.
2. V01: check v9 frames against today's live home page; cut before any section that changed; keep the page's own 'AI concept' style-preview label readable or cut those frames; append the 2.5 s true end card.
3. V50: make the v14 fixes in the v14 film project (end card, readable concept line wherever a concept frame remains, production publish address, risky names dropped, real image outputs swapped in), re-render on a local disk, then recompose the 15 s 9:16 (never crop the 16:9 master).
4. Music: Mixkit only off TikTok and only after the licence is re-read; TikTok gets a CML track.

### P11-built-in-makeborne: Built in Makeborne (the deliverable is the creative)

Used by: C07, C20, C38, C45; V39 and V43 number decks. Template: None: Makeborne's own deck/book export (PDF or PPTX), plus a T05 founder wrapper for the videos. Time: about 60 min the first time, 40 min after.

1. Run the brief on production exactly as written in creatives.json; keep the first output; add the end page in chat.
2. Check every slide for invented names, placeholder brackets and claims; numbers must trace to a source row kept outside this repo.
3. Export PDF (LinkedIn document) or capture slides for video; the 'Made with Makeborne' end page is the proof.

## 3. Template specs (build once, in `production/templates/`)

Each template is a HyperFrames project skeleton that follows the composition contract: one paused GSAP timeline registered on `window.__timelines`, `data-*` timing, variables on the root `<html>`, media timed on the element itself, captions on one captions track, no `Date.now()` or unseeded randomness. Start a piece with `node production/tools/new-project.mjs <template> <id> --episode <n> --formats 4x5,16x9` (copies the shared brand CSS, components and fonts, writes `BRIEF.md` from this plan, builds the format variants). T01 was checked (`hyperframes check` ok) and snapshotted in 9:16 and 16:9; the others lint clean apart from the expected missing-media errors (captures are never committed) and structural warnings.

| Template | Purpose | Inputs (variables) | Length | Sizes | Registry blocks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| T01-endcard | True end card: mark, 'Make yours at makeborne.com', price, refund line, keyword CTA. In a Higgsfield cut the same words sit over the clip in T07 endplate.html | headLead, headAccent, priceLine, refundLine, ctaText | 2.5 s | 9:16, 4:5, 1:1, 16:9 | logo-brand-close (reference for the cascade; restyled to the flat mark); grain-overlay (optional, 2% grain) |
| T02-speedrun | Frame-0 output on a phone, composer, sped or real-time build with clock chip, 3 labels, one chat edit, publish, loop. Higgsfield cut: mount the T07 hook before frame 0 and the bridge/endplate slots at the times in the sheet | hookLine, briefText, timerLine, clockText, label1-3, editRequest, liveText, ctaText, phoneScrollSrc, composerSrc, buildSrc (+ data-playback-rate), editSrc, publishSrc, musicSrc, voSrc | 32 s | 9:16 master, 4:5, 16:9 | cursor-zoom-follow (auto punch-in to clicks); ui-focus-zoom (zoom to the edited section); number-wheel (clock digits, optional); kinetic-center-build (label build, optional); device-frame-stage (alternative phone stage) |
| T03-split | Before/after: stacked on 9:16, side by side on 16:9/1:1, onion skin to prove nothing else moved | headline, beforeLabel, afterLabel, pollText, mode (stack\|wipe\|onion), beforeSrc, afterSrc, musicSrc, voSrc | 27 s | 9:16, 4:5, 1:1, 16:9 | before-after-wipe; comparison-split |
| T04-showcase | Real outputs with their brief: 8 s scroll episode, 15 s slides (and photo-mode stills), 6 s triptych; i2v: an AI camera move over a real output (Higgsfield image-to-video take, letter-checked) with its brief and run-log footnote | sentence, series, footnote (run log), pageSrc, s1-s5 / f1-f5, brief, line, i2v: clipSrc, sentence, footnote, aiLabelText ('AI camera move on a real Makeborne output') | 8 / 14.6 / 6 s | 9:16, 4:5, 1:1, 16:9 (triptych) | parallax-zoom; pan-stations; swipe-rail; screen-flow-carousel |
| T05-founder | Founder A-roll with Day-N pill, real-number ledger count-up, b-roll inserts in the lower half, captions | dayLabel, hookLine, n1-n3 + labels, arollSrc, broll1Src, broll2Src | 45 s | 9:16, 4:5 (LinkedIn), 16:9 (X, YouTube) | count-up; lt-soft-pill (pill styling reference); caption-editorial-emphasis (caption restyle option) |
| T06-tutorial | Step chips over trimmed windows of one continuous capture, sped build step with clock chip, punch-ins, optional face-cam | title, step1-4, ctaText, clockText, captureSrc, faceSrc | 90 (V24: 540 at 16:9) s | 9:16, 16:9 | ui-focus-zoom; cursor-zoom-follow; marker-checklist-card (V20 checklist) |
| T07-cinematic-hook | Higgsfield clip slots: clip in, 'AI-generated scene' label, HyperFrames type over the clip, hard cut to the real UI. index.html = hook (1-3 s); compositions/bridge.html = scene or transition (0.5-3 s); compositions/match-to-ui.html = the shop becomes its website (1.5 s, the real site grows out of the window); compositions/endplate.html = the end card over a darkened clip (2-4 s) | hook: hookLead, hookAccent, aiLabelText, plateSrc, nextSrc, bridge: plateSrc, lineText, aiLabelText, match: plateSrc, siteSrc (real capture), sentence, aiLabelText, endplate: plateSrc, headLead, headAccent, priceLine, refundLine, ctaText, aiLabelText, every slot: data-media-start on the clip = the section's clip start | hook 1-3 / bridge 0.5-3 / match 1.5 / endplate 2-4 s | 9:16 (Higgsfield cuts are TikTok, Reels and Shorts), 4:5 recomposed only for Reels feed tests | focus-blur-resolve; beat-freeze-cut |
| T08-props | Honest props with invented names: inbox stack, e-mail thread, notification over a clock, crossed-out fake sale banner, comment card. In a Higgsfield cut (V26, V40) the prop is typed over the T07 hook clip instead of the canvas | m1-m4, sender, badge, line | 4 s | 9:16, 4:5 | notification-cascade; chat-message; typing-indicator |
| T09-stills | Every static: proof split, type only, carousel step card, end strip, plate (Higgsfield frame grab behind the type, AI label on); one page per second, exported with snapshot | page markup per creative (COMPOSE text from creatives.json), capture PNGs, lay-plate: a Higgsfield frame grab (never a product screen) + the creative's headline | 1 per page s | 1080x1350, 1080x1080, 1200x1200, 1080x1920, 1600x900, 1920x1080, 1200x630, 1280x720, 1270x760 | grain-overlay (2%) |
| T10-ugc-pack | Creator finishing pass for the Spark/partnership version: in-video ad disclosure, captions, end card with code | adText, handle, code, creatorSrc | 45 s | 9:16 | none needed |
| T11-film-recut | Wrap a finished film render with a hook line, the optional AI-concept line and the T01 end card | filmSrc, hookLine, showConceptLine | 17 s | 9:16, 4:5 (from the 5:4 master project, recomposed), 16:9 | none (end card is T01 as a sub-composition) |

**Shared components** (`production/shared/components/`): flat mark, sentence chip, clock chip (`Real time · m:ss` / `Sped up ×N`), keyword CTA, AI label (`AI-generated scene`), concept line (`Projects shown are AI-generated concepts.`), ad disclosure (`Ad · Paid partnership with Makeborne`). **Tools** (`production/tools/`): `new-project.mjs`, `make-formats.mjs` (the v9 pattern: edit the master, regenerate 4:5, 1:1 and 16:9; recompose, never crop), `captions-to-html.mjs` (transcript to the captions sub-composition).

## 3a. Higgsfield: the plan

**Role.** Planned, not optional: Higgsfield makes the cinematic seconds (the scroll-stopping first 1-3 s, mood scenes, transitions, seasonal beats and slow camera moves over real outputs); HyperFrames makes every word, timer, label and the brand, and assembles the clips with the real captures; the real product is the proof in every video.

| Job | How | Shots |
|---|---|---|
| Scroll-stopping hooks (first 1-3 s) | A text-free clip with the hook line typed over it in HyperFrames (T07 hook), then a hard cut to the real product. | [HF01](#hf01) [HF03](#hf03) [HF05](#hf05) [HF09](#hf09) [HF16](#hf16) [HF17](#hf17) [HF18](#hf18) [HF19](#hf19) [HF20](#hf20) [HF21](#hf21) [HF22](#hf22) |
| Mood scenes: the shop at dawn becoming its website, the bookshelf, the pitch room | T07 match-to-ui grows the REAL published site out of the shop window; bridge and endplate slots carry the other scenes. | [HF03](#hf03) [HF04](#hf04) [HF07](#hf07) [HF11](#hf11) |
| Transitions | Short camera moves (through a window, along a street) as 0.5-1.5 s bridge slots between real scenes. | [HF02](#hf02) |
| Seasonal beats (Black Friday without a fake sale, the December holiday, the new year) | Scene + HyperFrames type: the calm shop while the street shouts; the December window; the new-year desk and street. | [HF14](#hf14) [HF23](#hf23) [HF24](#hf24) [HF25](#hf25) |
| Image-to-video of REAL Makeborne outputs | Start frame = a published site, slide or book page the product rendered; the model only moves the camera; kept only if every letter matches; labelled 'AI camera move on a real Makeborne output' (T04 i2v). | [HF06](#hf06) [HF08](#hf08) [HF10](#hf10) [HF12](#hf12) [HF13](#hf13) [HF15](#hf15) |

**Best model per job:**

| Job | Model | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Hero scenes re-used in several videos (pitch room, the roastery) | Higgsfield Cinema Studio (camera, lens and light control) | Best control of lens and light for the two shots that carry the most videos; 50 credits a 5 s take, so only 2 takes. |
| Hooks, scenes, transitions, seasonal beats | Kling 3.0 in Higgsfield, 1080p | Realistic, steady camera moves for 12.5 credits a 5 s take; most shots. |
| Image-to-video of real outputs | Kling 3.0 image-to-video (start frame), camera move only | Holds the start frame well; still checked letter by letter. |
| Physics that Kling gets wrong (paper, liquid, cloth) | Seedance 2.0 (fallback from the reserve) | Better motion physics, about 60 credits a 5 s take at 1080p. |
| Never | Veo with audio, Marketing Studio UGC avatars, Speak / lip-sync, Soul ID of a person, Click to Ad | We do not need generated audio; avatars and talking people are the AI tell this brand avoids, and an AI 'customer' is illegal as a testimonial. |

**Camera presets.** Camera preset names as listed on higgsfield.ai/camera-controls (read 26 Oct 2026): Dolly In, Dolly Out, Dolly Left, Dolly Right, Super Dolly In, Crane Down, Through Object In, Static and others. Pick the same-named move in the model's camera menu; where the model has no menu (or the name differs), the move is already written into the first sentence of the prompt.

**Every shot.** 9:16, 1080p, no generated audio (music comes from the platform libraries). Copy the prompt and the negative exactly. Record prompt, negative, model, preset, seed, take, credits, plan and date in the sidecar .json and the rights log. Download only the paid-plan, unwatermarked file to the private library (never this repository).

**Hard rules (never broken):**

- Never generate fake product UI or fake outputs and present them as real. Every app screen and every output on screen is a real capture; a generated clip never shows a screen with content.
- Never an AI avatar or any synthetic person as a 'customer', user or creator, and no testimonial of any kind from a generated person (FTC 16 CFR 465; the ASA applies its testimonial rules to synthetic people). In practice: no people at all in our generations.
- An on-screen label on every generated second ('AI-generated scene'; 'AI camera move on a real Makeborne output' for image-to-video), plus the platform AI labels (TikTok AI-generated content, YouTube altered or synthetic, Meta AI label) and the EU AI Act Art. 50 first-exposure disclosure.
- Never upload unreleased UI, client material, customer data or creator footage to Higgsfield: its standard terms allow training on inputs and outputs. The only uploads are image-to-video start frames that are outputs of live features made on the clean marketing account for the video itself (published sites, exported slides and book pages with fictional, trademark-checked names): material that is about to be posted anyway.
- Only live features are shown as live. Shots for beat videos (V51, V55, V56, V58) are generated only after their feature passes the six-box gate.

### The mix, and why

**Target:** In a video with Higgsfield: about 60-70% real product footage, 20-30% Higgsfield scenes, the rest HyperFrames-only cards; every letter, timer and the brand is HyperFrames, over everything.

**Measured on this plan:** 15 Higgsfield videos, 388 s: 273 s real (70%), 108 s Higgsfield (28%), 7 s HyperFrames-only cards (2%). Across all 60 videos (3023 s, including the founder, creator, tutorial and proof videos that stay real-only on purpose) Higgsfield is about 3.6% of posted seconds.

| Video | Length | Real s | Higgsfield s | HyperFrames-only s | Higgsfield % | When |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [V02](#v02) | 32 | 23 | 9 | 0 | 28% | now |
| [V03](#v03) | 30 | 22 | 8 | 0 | 27% | now |
| [V07](#v07) | 40 | 28.5 | 11.5 | 0 | 29% | now |
| [V14](#v14) | 27 | 20 | 7 | 0 | 26% | now |
| [V15](#v15) | 24 | 17 | 7 | 0 | 29% | now |
| [V16](#v16) | 15 | 9 | 4 | 2 | 27% | now |
| [V18](#v18) | 15 | 10.5 | 4.5 | 0 | 30% | now |
| [V25](#v25) | 30 | 22 | 8 | 0 | 27% | now |
| [V26](#v26) | 20 | 14 | 6 | 0 | 30% | now |
| [V27](#v27) | 35 | 25.5 | 9.5 | 0 | 27% | now |
| [V29](#v29) | 8 | 5 | 2 | 1 | 25% | now |
| [V40](#v40) | 12 | 5 | 3 | 4 | 25% | now |
| [V41](#v41) | 30 | 21 | 9 | 0 | 30% | now |
| [V42](#v42) | 45 | 32.5 | 12.5 | 0 | 28% | now |
| [V48](#v48) | 25 | 18 | 7 | 0 | 28% | now |
| [V51](#v51) | 25 | 19.5 | 5.5 | 0 | 22% | only once its feature is live |
| [V55](#v55) | 20 | 15.5 | 4.5 | 0 | 23% | only once its feature is live |
| [V56](#v56) | 35 | 29.5 | 5.5 | 0 | 16% | only once its feature is live |
| [V58](#v58) | 45 | 37.5 | 7.5 | 0 | 17% | only once its feature is live |
| [V59](#v59) | 50 | 43 | 7 | 0 | 14% | only once its feature is live |

- Real product footage is the proof and what converts in this category: the visible UI, the finished output and a next step (LightReel, Oct 2026); text-plus-product statics lead Meta hit rates (Motion 2026). So it stays the majority of every video (60-70%).
- Higgsfield buys the first seconds and the feeling that screen recordings lack: the café at dawn, the empty pitch room, the bookshelf. That is where scroll-stopping happens, so it gets a real share (20-30%), not a token 2 s.
- It stays capped: fully AI-made ads did worse on sales impact than human-made ones (Ipsos and Syracuse, May 2026) and visible AI tells cause backlash (NN/G, Dec 2025). Above about 30% the product stops being the star.
- All text, timing and the brand are HyperFrames because generated video cannot be trusted with letters, and the timers ('Real time · m:ss', 'Sped up ×N') and prices must be exact.
- The founder, creator, tutorial and proof videos stay real-only: trust there comes from a real face, real numbers and an uncut capture.

### A/B: real vs Higgsfield

**Metric:** /go/ link clicks per 1,000 views and tracked sales per 1,000 views, each version's first 7 days, same surface. Never views alone. **Weekly shift:** Every Sunday: if the Higgsfield side of a pair (or the Higgsfield videos as a group) is 20%+ better on clicks per 1,000 views with sales per 1,000 views not lower, next week's new videos move 5 points toward Higgsfield (up to 30% of seconds); if 20%+ worse, 5 points back toward real (down to real-only); otherwise hold. Organic posts on different days are not a controlled test: treat one pair as directional, three pairs pointing the same way as a decision.

| Pair | Item | Surface | Real | Higgsfield | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| AB1 | [V07](#v07) | TikTok | 2026-12-07 /go/tt-v07-e04 | 2026-11-01 /go/tt-v07 | Higgsfield first, real second (counterbalances the order of the other pairs). |
| AB2 | [V29](#v29) | YouTube Shorts | 2026-11-04 /go/yt-v29 + 2026-11-30 /go/yt-v29-e04 | 2026-11-19 /go/yt-v29-e02 + 2026-11-23 /go/yt-v29-e03 | Two episodes a side (image-to-video of the real output vs the plain sentence chip). |
| AB3 | [V03](#v03) | TikTok | 2026-10-28 /go/tt-v03 | 2026-11-24 /go/tt-v03-e03 | Episode 3 has a new brief; the format and hook line stay the same. |
| AB4 | [V02](#v02) | TikTok | 2026-10-28 /go/tt-v02 | 2026-11-25 /go/tt-v02-e04 | Episode 4 has a new brief; the bakery clip is the opening and end in the Higgsfield version. |
| AB5 | [V40](#v40) | TikTok | 2026-11-27 /go/tt-v40 | 2026-11-30 /go/tt-v40-e02 | Black Friday (real) vs Cyber Monday (Higgsfield). Same offer, same words. |
| AB6 | [V18](#v18) | TikTok | 2026-11-06 /go/tt-v18 | 2026-12-11 /go/tt-v18-e02 | Episode 2 has a new brief; same hook line. |
| AB7 | [C30](#c30) | Instagram Stories | 2026-11-27 /go/ig-c30 | 2026-11-30 /go/ig-c30-e02 | The typeset story vs the same words over the HF15 frame grab (T09 lay-plate). The 30 Nov story gets its own short link so the two can be told apart. |

### Details

#### V01

**The Fly-over, pinned: 'We launched today'** · P0 · week 1 · 17 s · first post 2026-10-29 21:30 TikTok · **readiness: now**

- **Make with:** real only. **Pipeline / template:** P10-film-recut / T11-film-recut. **Tools:** Makeborne (real run), OBS screen capture, HyperFrames, platform music library.
- **Capture in the app:**
  - No new filming. Open today's live makeborne.com home page in the 1440x900 window and screenshot it; compare with v9 frames at 0, 3, 6, 9, 12 s (`npx hyperframes snapshot --at` on the v9 project copy) and cut before any section that changed
  - Confirm the home page's style previews still carry their AI-concept label and that it is readable at phone size; otherwise cut those frames
  - 16:9: re-use the 3840x2160 render + the T01 end card at 16:9; 4:5: recompose from the 5:4 master project (copy, never crop the 16:9)
- **Template variables:** `filmSrc` = film library (private)/v9-flyover/renders/makeborne-v9-flyover-9x16-1080x1920-60fps.mp4 (copied); `hookLine` = We launched today. This is the real studio.; `showConceptLine` = false; `endcard` = {"headLead":"Make yours at","headAccent":"makeborne.com","priceLine":"From $20/mo + tax","refundLine":"Full refund until you use credits","ctaText":"TikTok: Link in bio, or DM me SITE · Instagram: Comment SITE · X/LinkedIn: Link in the first reply"}
- **On-screen text, exactly:**
  - 0-2s: "We launched today. This is the real studio."
  - 2-12s: "Websites. Books. Decks."
  - 12-14.5s: "One idea. Every possibility."
  - 14.5-17s: "makeborne.com · from $20/mo + tax · full refund until you use credits"
- **Audio:** music: IG/LinkedIn/X/YouTube: the film's Mixkit 'Opalescent' mix (re-read the licence before any paid use). TikTok: swap to a CML track of the same feel (bright, 140 BPM half-time). VO: None (music only); optional founder line 'We launched today.' on TikTok SFX: As in the film
- **Captions:** No speech: none. On-screen hook and end card carry it.
- **Disclosure:** None required (real home page). Keep the page's own 'AI concept' label on the style previews readable, or cut those frames
- **Render:** 9:16 1080x1920 (TikTok, Reels, Shorts: native re-edit each); 16:9 1920x1080 (X); 4:5 1080x1350 (LinkedIn, captions, no music); 4:5 1080x1350 (Instagram feed / Meta partnership-ad candidate, recomposed).
- **Time and cost:** capture 20 min, edit 70 min, about 105 min the first time (61 min once routine). Tool cost $0. Makeborne AI provider cost of the builds about $0.00 (outside the $1,000; plan.json outsideBudget).
- **Mix:** 17 s real, 0 s Higgsfield, 0 s HyperFrames-only.
- **P0 checks:** Home page in the film must still match the live page; if a section changed, cut before it · The page shows style previews labelled as AI concepts: keep that label readable or cut those frames · End card must not say free or trial

#### V02

**One brief in, a whole website out (bakery speedrun)** · P0 · week 1 · 32 s · first post 2026-10-29 22:00 TikTok · **readiness: now**

- **Make with:** Higgsfield hook + real. **Pipeline / template:** P1-speedrun / T02-speedrun. **Tools:** Makeborne (real run), OBS screen capture, HyperFrames, Higgsfield (Kling 3.0), platform music library.
- **Capture in the app:**
  - Composer in Website mode, 860x1080 portrait window, brief typed by hand: A website for a family bakery in Leeds that opens at 7.
  - Plan step visible, then Create; full uncut build recording (log start/end, credits)
  - One chat edit typed: make it warmer and put the opening hours at the top (record until only that section has changed)
  - Publish dialog, then the live Makeborne address open in a fresh browser tab
  - Phone: the published site at 390 px, a slow full-page scroll (frame 0 and the loop)
  - Check the bakery name the build invents against trademarks and real Leeds bakeries
- **Template variables:** `hookLine` = One brief in. A whole website out.; `briefText` = "A website for a family bakery in Leeds that opens at 7."; `timerLine` = Timer's on.; `clockText` = Real time · m:ss if the cut plays at 1x; otherwise 'Sped up ×N' (expect ×8 for a 2 min build in 15 s); `label1` = Headline.; `label2` = Menu.; `label3` = Opening hours.; `editRequest` = make it warmer and put the opening hours at the top; `liveText` = Live. Real time [m:ss from the run log].; `ctaText` = What should I build next? Comment it.
- **On-screen text, exactly:**
  - 0-2s: "One brief in. A whole website out." (VO: One brief. Watch.)
  - 2-5s: "Timer's on." (VO: I type a brief about a bakery.)
  - 5-20s: "Headline. / Menu. / Opening hours." (VO: It writes the page, picks the type, lays out the menu.)
  - 20-28s: "Live. [real time]" (VO: One edit, publish, and it's live on a Makeborne address.)
  - 28-32s: "What should I build next? Comment it." (VO: Tell me what to build next.)
- **Audio:** music: Brand TikTok: Commercial Music Library track, warm, upbeat acoustic-pop, about 118-124 BPM. Instagram/YouTube: a Meta Sound Collection / YouTube Audio Library track of the same mood. LinkedIn and X cuts: no music. VO: Founder's own voice, recorded to picture (voice memo, quiet room, 30 cm from the phone). Lines from the script: 'One brief. Watch.' / 'I type a brief about a bakery.' / 'It writes the page, picks the type, lays out the menu.' / 'One edit, publish, and it's live on a Makeborne address.' / 'Tell me what to build next.' SFX: Real UI sound only where it helps: one click on Create, a soft pop as each chip lands, the publish/export moment. No whooshes on every cut.
- **Captions:** Burned-in, from `hyperframes transcribe` -> captions-to-html.mjs, 3-4 words per group, white pill at y 1060 (9:16); SRT uploaded on YouTube
- **Disclosure:** Higgsfield version: On-screen 'AI-generated scene' chip on every frame of the clip (HyperFrames); TikTok 'AI-generated content' on; YouTube altered or synthetic content = Yes; Meta: add the AI label on organic posts (it may also add 'AI info' itself); the chip is also the EU AI Act first-exposure disclosure. Real version (A/B pair) and LinkedIn/X cuts: no AI label needed (real screen capture / real footage / typeset HTML). Keep the 'Sped up ×N' chip honest.
- **Render:** 9:16 1080x1920 (TikTok, Reels, Shorts: native re-edit each); 16:9 1920x1080 (X); 4:5 1080x1350 (Instagram feed / Meta partnership-ad candidate, recomposed).
- **Time and cost:** capture 45 min, edit 120 min, about 180 min the first time (106 min once routine). Tool cost 75 Higgsfield credits (about $3.68 at Plus, production budget). Makeborne AI provider cost of the builds about $0.60 (outside the $1,000; plan.json outsideBudget). Higgsfield credits come from the production budget, never the $1,000.
- **Mix:** 23 s real, 9 s Higgsfield, 0 s HyperFrames-only.

## 5. Image production sheet (all 48)

**What each still is made of.** Real product screenshot composited in a HyperFrames still: 36. HyperFrames-rendered typographic still: 8. Built inside Makeborne (the export is the creative): 4. AI-generated art in the v14 reference style: **0**. Higgsfield frame grabs behind the type (never a product screen): C28 and C30, in their Higgsfield versions only. The v14 sheet stays the internal quality bar for the images beat; posting concept art as if Makeborne made it would show a coming feature as live. After the images beat, image-led creatives use real outputs of the shipped product, which carry the product's own machine-readable marking.

## 6. The weekly rhythm

| Day | Block (Sarajevo) | Job |
|---|---|---|
| Mon | 10:30-12:30 + 14:00-15:00 | Capture day A: every screen capture for the week's new videos (builds recorded uncut), plus the outputs needed for the week's stills |
| Tue | 09:00-10:00 + 10:30-12:30 + evening | Higgsfield block 09:00-10:00 (the week's shots from the sheet, prompts copied exactly), then the edit day: T02/T03/T06 cuts of Monday's captures with the Higgsfield slots mounted, captions, first renders |
| Wed | 10:30-12:30 | Stills day: statics, carousels, stories, X cards (T09); LinkedIn documents built in Makeborne |
| Thu | 10:30-12:30 | Capture day B: founder face-to-camera batch, comment-reply builds from Mon-Wed requests, beat captures when a gate passes |
| Fri | 10:30-12:30 + evening | Re-cut and render: the week's winner re-cuts with 2 new hooks, native re-edits per platform, QA against the quality bar, schedule natively |
| Sat | optional, 1-2 h | Bank: next week's founder takes, beat prep, carousel episodes |
| Sun | Numbers 08:00, review 90 min | Day-N numbers video (real figures), Sunday review ritual: pick winners for Friday re-cuts; A/B read of the real vs Higgsfield versions (/go/ clicks and sales per 1,000 views) and next week's mix |

**Output per week, from the calendar** (a production = a new brief and output, never a re-upload; re-edits are native versions for other surfaces):

| Week | Dates | New videos | New stills | Beat items (on gate) | Re-edits | Winner re-cuts | Creator drafts | Est. hours | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 2026-10-26 to 2026-11-01 | 10 | 14 | 0 | 13 | 0 | 0 | 40 | Launch-week crash: templates are built while producing (see launchWeek); expect long days |
| 2 | 2026-11-02 to 2026-11-08 | 10 | 11 | 0 | 8 | 3 | 0 | 23.4 | Above the ~12-16 h/week make budget: bank founder takes on Saturday, cut X-only extras and long YouTube first (strategy section 18) |
| 3 | 2026-11-09 to 2026-11-15 | 10 | 7 | 0 | 8 | 3 | 5 | 25.1 | Above the ~12-16 h/week make budget: bank founder takes on Saturday, cut X-only extras and long YouTube first (strategy section 18) |
| 4 | 2026-11-16 to 2026-11-22 | 2 | 1 | 12 | 1 | 3 | 7 | 16.5 | Above the ~12-16 h/week make budget: bank founder takes on Saturday, cut X-only extras and long YouTube first (strategy section 18) |
| 5 | 2026-11-23 to 2026-11-29 | 11 | 7 | 0 | 8 | 3 | 2 | 23.8 | Above the ~12-16 h/week make budget: bank founder takes on Saturday, cut X-only extras and long YouTube first (strategy section 18) |
| 6 | 2026-11-30 to 2026-12-06 | 10 | 5 | 2 | 6 | 3 | 1 | 21.5 | Above the ~12-16 h/week make budget: bank founder takes on Saturday, cut X-only extras and long YouTube first (strategy section 18) |
| 7 | 2026-12-07 to 2026-12-13 | 10 | 4 | 3 | 4 | 3 | 1 | 23.7 | Above the ~12-16 h/week make budget: bank founder takes on Saturday, cut X-only extras and long YouTube first (strategy section 18) |
| 8 | 2026-12-14 to 2026-12-20 | 8 | 4 | 5 | 6 | 3 | 0 | 24.9 | Above the ~12-16 h/week make budget: bank founder takes on Saturday, cut X-only extras and long YouTube first (strategy section 18) |
| 9 | 2026-12-21 to 2026-12-27 | 5 | 3 | 1 | 4 | 0 | 0 | 9.9 | Fits the routine's make blocks |
| 10 | 2026-12-28 to 2027-01-03 | 5 | 4 | 0 | 5 | 0 | 0 | 10 | Fits the routine's make blocks |
| 11 | 2027-01-04 to 2027-01-10 | 7 | 5 | 1 | 8 | 3 | 0 | 17.9 | Above the ~12-16 h/week make budget: bank founder takes on Saturday, cut X-only extras and long YouTube first (strategy section 18) |
| 12 | 2027-01-11 to 2027-01-17 | 7 | 5 | 4 | 5 | 3 | 0 | 20.5 | Above the ~12-16 h/week make budget: bank founder takes on Saturday, cut X-only extras and long YouTube first (strategy section 18) |
| 13 | 2027-01-18 to 2027-01-25 | 9 | 7 | 2 | 7 | 3 | 0 | 25.7 | Above the ~12-16 h/week make budget: bank founder takes on Saturday, cut X-only extras and long YouTube first (strategy section 18) |

**The honest finding:** at the calendar's volume, production needs about 20-25 hours a week after launch week, against the routine's 10-12 hours of make blocks plus evenings. Ways to close the gap, in order: (1) batch strictly (Mon screen, Thu face, Wed stills), (2) cut long YouTube and X-only extras first (strategy section 18), (3) lean on the six-cuts-per-capture rule in section 10, (4) bring the editor hire forward from revenue (P-D11). Do not close it by lowering the truth rules.

## 7. Quality bar (before anything posts)

**Hook**

- [ ] Frame 0 shows the finished output, a face or one bold line: never black, never a logo
- [ ] The proposition is understandable muted within 1 s and fully by 3 s
- [ ] Hook text is 3-7 words and sits inside the safe band

**Safe zones**

- [ ] 9:16: all words and the product UI between y 270 and 1250 px, x 65-950 px (TikTok right rail and caption block; Reels/Stories 14% top, 35% bottom, 6% sides) [U: re-check each platform's template monthly]
- [ ] Stories: nothing in the bottom 35% (link sticker)
- [ ] 4:5 and 1:1: 7% margins; 16:9: 80% title-safe for type
- [ ] YouTube thumbnail: keep the bottom-right 180x60 px clear (timestamp)
- [ ] Read the snapshot --review phone sheet at 360 px wide

**Type and captions**

- [ ] Readable text at least 5.5% of frame height on video, held at least max(0.83 s, 0.3 s per word)
- [ ] Burned-in captions on every video with speech; 3-5 words per group; brand words spelled right (Makeborne, Sarajevo, PPTX)
- [ ] One Newsreader italic accent word per piece; the brand gradient only there and on the Create button in captures; coral at most once

**Sound**

- [ ] Brand TikTok: Commercial Music Library only; IG/YT: their libraries; LinkedIn/X: no music
- [ ] Integrated loudness -14 LUFS (±1), true peak at or below -1 dBTP; music under voice around 0.14 volume
- [ ] SFX only on visible actions; rotate takes; no synthesised dings

**Truth**

- [ ] Every product frame comes from a real run on production; no reconstructed or fake UI in product proof
- [ ] No coming feature shown as live: images, image editing, custom domains, two-way GitHub sync, top-ups, agency tools, AI lead research stay off screen until each passes the six-box gate
- [ ] Builds sped up carry the 'Sped up ×N' chip; 'Real time' only at 1x; times from the run log, never rounded down
- [ ] No 'free', 'trial' or 'try free'; price line 'From $20/mo + tax'; refund line exactly 'Full refund until you use credits' (update everywhere if D1 changes it)
- [ ] Publish address is whatever production really issues ('a Makeborne address'), never 'your own domain' before the domains beat
- [ ] Books: 'for screen and PDF', never 'print-ready'; decks text-led until images ship
- [ ] No speed, user, revenue or income claims; no competitor scores or 'better than'
- [ ] Numbers in founder videos typed from the source on the day, refunds included

**People, names and data**

- [ ] Fictional, trademark-checked business names only (or a real business with written OK, public info only, unpublished by default)
- [ ] No personal data: buyer names, e-mails, handles (unless agreed), lead-finder contacts blurred
- [ ] Consent on file (private log, never in this repo) for anyone on camera or quoted
- [ ] No real child's face; no real person made to say anything

**Disclosure**

- [ ] Paid creators: 'Ad' said and shown in the first 3 s, '#ad' first in the caption, paid-partnership toggle on
- [ ] Any generated shot: 'AI-generated scene' chip on every frame; TikTok AIGC toggle and YouTube altered/synthetic = Yes when realistic
- [ ] v13/v14 concept frames: 'Projects shown are AI-generated concepts.' readable (cap height >= 2.5% of frame, >= 3 s)
- [ ] Generated images inside real outputs (after the images beat): an on-screen note 'Images made by Makeborne's AI', the platform toggle when photoreal, and the product's machine-readable marking (gate box 6)
- [ ] No AI avatar, synthetic voice or invented 'customer' anywhere; testimonials only from real customers in their own words

**Higgsfield shots**

- [ ] The prompt, negative, model, preset and takes match the shot card exactly; seed and credits are in the sidecar .json and the rights log
- [ ] No person, face or hand; no letter, number or sign; no screen with content; no real business or logo (checked frame by frame)
- [ ] Image-to-video: every letter and block matches the real output at 100% zoom on the first, middle and last frame
- [ ] The 'AI-generated scene' (or 'AI camera move on a real Makeborne output') chip is on for every generated frame, and the post has the platform AI label on
- [ ] Only the paid-plan, unwatermarked file is used; nothing unreleased or client-owned was uploaded

**Brand system**

- [ ] Canvas #F8F7F4, ink #18181B, secondary #5C616D, hairline #E4E1DA
- [ ] Inter display 640-720, tracking -0.05 em; Newsreader italic accent
- [ ] Flat m only (glossy m only in the films), never stretched, rotated, shadowed or gradient-filled; clear space 1/5 of its width
- [ ] Anti-slop: no purple-blue gradient fields, glowing prompt pills, glass, orbs, particles, lens flares, morphing text, walls of cards
- [ ] Frame never parks: every hold drifts 0.5-1%/s

**Tracking and file**

- [ ] Unique utm_content per cut (surface-id-hook), /go/ link in bio/first comment/first reply, never a Whop store or tracking link
- [ ] File named per section 9 and logged in the posted register with caption, link, track ID and disclosure state
- [ ] `npx hyperframes check --json` clean of errors; render duration equals the root data-duration (ffprobe)

## 8. Folder and file naming

- **Files:** `<id>-e<episode>_<surface>_<cut>_<hook>_<W>x<H>_v<NN>.<ext>`
- **Surfaces:** `tt` TikTok, `ig` Instagram Reels, `igf` Instagram feed, `igs` Instagram Stories, `yt` YouTube Shorts, `ytl` YouTube long, `li` LinkedIn, `x` X, `ih` Indie Hackers, `ph` Product Hunt, `c` creator
- **Cuts:** `punch` 25-35 s main cut, `full` 60-150 s full build, `loop` 8-15 s loop, `recut` winner re-cut with a new hook, `b` cinematic-hook B-variant, `still` static or carousel page, `cover` cover frame
- **Examples:** `V02-e1_tt_punch_r3_1080x1920_v02.mp4`, `V02-e1_li_full_r3_1080x1350_v01.mp4`, `V18-e1_tt_b_w2_1080x1920_v01.mp4`, `C05-e1_igf_still-p03_1080x1350_v01.png`
- **UTM:** utm_content = <surface>-<id lower-case>-<hookCode>[b] (for example tt-v02-r3, tt-v02-r3b for the B-variant), matching content/tracking.md
- **Captures:** <date>_<id>_<screen>_<take>.mp4, for example 2026-10-26_V02_build-uncut_t1.mp4; run log row per build: date, id, brief, start, end, m:ss, credits, output URL (private)
- **Generations:** <date>_<id>_hf_<model>_<preset>_<take>.mp4 with a sidecar .json: prompt, model, preset, duration, aspect, credits, plan, label status
- **Projects:** `production/projects/<id>-e<episode>/` (gitignored), created by `new-project.mjs`; format variants in `formats/<4x5|1x1|16x9>/`; renders in `renders/`.

## 9. Asset library structure

Private, on a local disk (not the network drive, not this repo). Mirror weekly to the owner's private backup.

```
00-admin/            consent-log (private), rights-log.csv (track IDs, licences, AI tool + plan + date per generated shot), run-log.csv (brief, time, credits per build), posted-register.csv
01-capture/<week>/<id>/   raw OBS and phone captures, re-encoded -g 60 copies
02-outputs/<brief-slug>/  real outputs: 390-wide full-page PNG, 1440x900 hero, slides, book pages, PPTX/PDF exports, with the run-log row
03-face/<week>/           founder takes, best take marked _best
04-gen/<id>/              generated plates (B-variants only) + prompt sidecars; never product imagery
05-audio/                 vo/<id>.wav, music/<platform>/<trackID>.wav (TikTok CML in-app only), sfx/
06-projects/              HyperFrames projects created by production/tools/new-project.mjs (or symlink to production/projects/)
07-renders/<id>/          final MP4s per surface and size, named per the convention
08-stills/<Cid>/          PNG/JPG/PDF exports per creative
09-posted/<date>/         the exact file posted + caption + link + utm_content + disclosure state
99-reference/             film library renders (read-only copies): v9 9:16/16:9/5:4, v14 draft 1, v12 real deck and book pages, v14 reference sheet (quality bar only)
```

## 10. Review loop and repurposing

### Review loop

| Step | Who | What |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Maker (founder now; editor once hired from revenue) | Self-check against the quality bar while building; `hyperframes check --json` clean; `snapshot --review` sheets read (beats, seams, phone). |
| 2 | Maker | Watch the render once muted on a phone and once with sound; check the clock chip factor against data-playback-rate and the run log. |
| 3 | Owner (second pair of eyes) | Required for: every P0, every paid boost or Spark candidate, every launch-beat asset, every creator draft, every founder numbers post. 10-minute review in the HyperFrames preview or on the MP4; comments go back as frame-id + time. |
| 4 | Maker | Fix, re-render only the changed frame/scene, log in the posted register, schedule natively with the right toggles. |
| 5 | Founder | 48 h read: 3-second hold, completion, /go/ clicks per 1,000 views, DMs. Sunday ritual: pick the top 10% for winner re-cuts with 2 new hooks; retire the bottom 50% of formats (strategy section 18). |

### One capture, six cuts

Every capture session is planned for six cuts before recording, so one uncut build pays for a week of posts.

| # | Cut | Made from | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 25-35 s punch cut, 9:16, TikTok (brand) | T02 with the build at ×N | TikTok engagement peaks under 30 s |
| 2 | Native Reels re-edit, 9:16 | same project: new cover frame, keyword CTA 'Comment SITE', no TikTok watermark, Meta library music | Never upload the TikTok export to Instagram |
| 3 | YouTube Shorts loop cut, 9:16, 20-30 s | same project: last frame matches frame 0 so replays count | Shorts count replays as views |
| 4 | 60-150 s full build, 9:16 and 4:5 | same capture at 1x-2x: TikTok long and LinkedIn with captions, no music on LinkedIn | 120-180 s TikToks got about 10x the views (Socialinsider H1 2026) |
| 5 | 16:9 X cut, 20-45 s, first-line hook in the post | make-formats 16x9 | 16:9 outperformed 9:16 on X launch videos (Infinite, 2026) |
| 6 | Stills: 4:5 proof static, carousel page, story, OG/landing loop, 8 s 'Made from one brief' episode, cover | T09 and T04 from the same output | Text-plus-product statics lead Meta hit rate (Motion 2026) |

## 11. Rights and disclosure rules

Real screen captures of our own product, the founder filming himself and typeset HTML need no AI label. Labels are required for (1) every Higgsfield second ('AI-generated scene' on screen plus the platform label), (2) image-to-video camera moves over real outputs ('AI camera move on a real Makeborne output'), (3) concept art shown in films, (4) generated images inside outputs after the images beat, and (5) every paid creator post. A fake customer testimonial by an AI avatar is never allowed.

| Where | Rule | Source |
|---|---|---|
| TikTok | Turn on the AI-generated content label for any post with a realistic generated scene or person (Community Guidelines effective 24 Sep 2026). Brand account uses the Commercial Music Library only. Paid creators: branded-content toggle plus 'Ad' in video. | https://www.tiktok.com/community-guidelines/en/integrity-authenticity (released 2026-08-25, effective 2026-09-24) |
| Instagram / Facebook (Meta) | Meta adds 'AI info' automatically for content made with its own generative tools or detected through industry signals (C2PA); it appears under 'About this ad'. Use Meta's AI label on organic posts with realistic generated shots. Paid creators: paid-partnership label. | https://about.fb.com/news/2025/02/gen-ai-transparency-metas-ads-products/ (2025-02-03, updated 2026-06-01) |
| YouTube | Altered or synthetic content = Yes for photorealistic generated scenes; not needed for captions, upscaling, AI-written scripts, our own real captures or a non-realistic stylised plate. Label shows on the player for photorealistic content. | https://support.google.com/youtube/answer/14328491 (read 2026-10-26) |
| EU AI Act Art. 50 | From 2 Aug 2026: a generated image or video that resembles real people, places or events and could falsely appear authentic is a deepfake: label it clearly at first exposure (the final Code of Practice of 10 Jun 2026 points to an EU icon or equivalent label). Evidently artistic or fictional shots get a lighter disclosure. Makeborne as a provider must also mark its own generated images machine-readably once images ship (gate box 6). | https://www.jonesday.com/en/insights/2026/06/european-commission-publishes-final-code-of-practice-on-marking-and-labelling-aigenerated-content (2026-06) |
| FTC (US) / ASA (UK) | Testimonials must come from real people with real experience; AI-generated reviewers and fake customers are banned (16 CFR 465), and the ASA applies its testimonial rules to deepfakes and synthetic people. Creator posts disclose the material connection in the video itself. Credits for sharing reward the act of sharing, never positive sentiment. | https://www.ftc.gov/news-events/news/press-releases/2024/08/federal-trade-commission-announces-final-rule-banning-fake-reviews-testimonials (2024-08-14 (in force Oct 2024)) |

**Never allowed:**

- An AI avatar or synthetic person presented as a customer, user or creator
- Our own team posing as unconnected customers
- A cloned voice of anyone but the founder (and only with his consent and a description note)
- Generated 'screenshots' of Makeborne output presented as real output
- A real person or business shown endorsing Makeborne without written consent
- Uploading unreleased UI, client material, customer data or creator footage to Higgsfield or any generator whose terms allow training
- A generated clip that shows a screen, an app or an output and is passed off as Makeborne

**A fake 'customer' testimonial by an AI avatar is not allowed, in any form, on any surface, paid or organic.** The FTC rule bans AI-generated testimonials from people who do not exist, and the ASA applies its testimonial rules to synthetic people. Higgsfield's UGC-avatar and Click-to-Ad features are therefore out of scope for this brand.

**Music rights:** brand TikTok uses the Commercial Music Library only; Instagram/Facebook and YouTube use their own libraries on their own platforms; Mixkit 'Opalescent' only after the licence is re-read, never on TikTok ads. Log every track ID in the rights log.

**Generated material:** keep the prompt, model, plan, date and credits for every generated shot; download only paid-plan (unwatermarked) files; never upload unreleased UI, client material, customer data or creator footage to a generator whose terms allow training.

## 12. Production budget and costs

**Production budget (separate from the $1,000 ads and growth budget).** Production money (Higgsfield) and the $1,000 ads and growth budget are two different pots. Nothing in this budget is logged in the Ads spend log, the ads.json calendar or plan.json; nothing in the $1,000 ever pays for a production tool. The owner buys it and signs up himself, on his own card, monthly (not annual). Nobody else buys, signs up, generates or posts on his behalf.

- **Recommended tier:** Higgsfield Plus, monthly, $49/month for 1,000 credits. Higgsfield's own blog 'Higgsfield pricing explained' (published 24 Oct 2026, read 26 Oct 2026): Plus $49/month, 1,000 credits, commercial use. Higgsfield's comparison post of 19 Oct 2026 lists the same $15 / $49 / $129 monthly prices.
- **Conflicting figures:** Krea's capture (updated 23 Oct 2026) lists Plus at $59 monthly ($47 annual) with 1,200 credits and Starter at $19 / 270; Creatify lists lower prices. higgsfield.ai/pricing renders in JavaScript and could not be read on 26 Oct. The checkout page is final: if it shows $59 for 1,200 credits, that is still the right tier (the cap below allows it). Ultra is $129 / 3,000 credits in every source.
- **Why this tier:** Month 1 plans 700 credits of shots (+100 only if images, top-ups or domains go live) against 1,000. Starter (200 credits) would not even cover week 1 (313 credits). Ultra (3,000 credits for $129) would leave about two thirds unused: buy it only if the Higgsfield versions clearly win and the mix moves up.
- **Not this:** Not annual (no commitment before the lift is measured). No credit packs or auto-refill in month 1: if credits run out, the remaining shots wait, or drop to Kling 3.0 at 720p (10 credits a take). Not the API (pay-as-you-go needs integration work for no gain here). Never Marketing Studio UGC avatars, Speak or lip-sync, Soul ID of people, or 'Click to Ad'.
- **Month 1 (2026-10-27 to 2026-11-26):** Higgsfield Plus, monthly (owner signs up Tue 27 Oct) $49. Total **$49** (cap $59: Cap $59 in case checkout shows Krea's higher Plus price. Anything above the cap needs the owner's own new decision.)
- **Credits, month 1:** 1000 available, 700 planned, 100 only if beat features go live, 200 reserve (retakes and the Seedance 2.0 fallback (about 60 credits a take) if a physics shot fails on Kling). Kling 3.0: 3 takes in month 1 while the prompts are new, 2 takes from month 2. Cinema Studio: 2 takes (hero shots that are re-used). Image-to-video: 2 takes. Stop at the first take that passes the keep rule.
